| 413 | ### 10. Sole proprietor with municipal surtax | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 415 | Principal-business taxable profit 20,000; the municipality's surtax decision sets 13%. PIT is 324 + 8,000 × 15% = 1,524. Surtax is 13% of the PIT, 198.12. Total 1,722.12. Contributions are computed separately. [PIT Law art. 10(1)(2)][law]; [Local Self-Government Financing Law arts. 8 and 9][local] | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 417 | ```oa-check | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 418 | name: Montenegro principal business PIT with municipal surtax | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 419 | inputs: | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 420 |   taxable_profit: 20000 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 421 |   surtax_rate: 0.13 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 422 | steps: | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 423 |   - pit: max(min(taxable_profit, 12000) - 8400, 0) * 0.09 + max(taxable_profit - 12000, 0) * 0.15 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 424 |   - surtax: round(pit * surtax_rate, 2) | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 425 |   - total: pit + surtax | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 426 | expected: | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 427 |   pit: 1524 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 428 |   surtax: 198.12 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 429 |   total: 1722.12 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 430 | ``` | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 432 | ### 11. Gambling winning before and after 21 May 2026 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 434 | A betting winning of 1,500 after deducting the stake. Paid on 10 March 2026, the bands apply: 1,450 × 10% = 145. Paid on 10 June 2026, the flat rate applies: 1,500 × 12% = 180. The organiser withholds and pays by the 15th of the next month. [PIT Law arts. 10(1)(4), 37s and 50f][law]; [70/26 arts. 1 and 3][may] | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 436 | ```oa-check | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 437 | name: Montenegro gambling winning before and after the 70/26 change | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 438 | inputs: | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 439 |   winning: 1500 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 440 | steps: | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 441 |   - tax_before_21_may: max(min(winning, 1500) - 50, 0) * 0.10 + max(winning - 1500, 0) * 0.15 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 442 |   - tax_from_21_may: winning * 0.12 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 443 | expected: | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 444 |   tax_before_21_may: 145 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 445 |   tax_from_21_may: 180 | Retained examples appendix: unchanged exact line |

| 446 | ``` | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 448 | ## Ask the client first | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 450 | 1. Residence: domicile, centre of vital interests, days in Montenegro in 2026, and any posting abroad or digital-nomad status. | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 451 | 2. Which income classes: salary (and from how many payers), registered business as main or supplementary activity, occasional contracts, rent (and whether to tourists, with a licence), interest and dividends, sales of property, stakes or shares, sport, royalties, internet or gaming income, gambling winnings. | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 452 | 3. For a business: accounts, fixed-asset register, unused losses by year, advances paid, VAT registration, lump-sum decision, and any underdeveloped-municipality or other relief decision. | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 453 | 4. Municipality of activity or residence, and its surtax decision. | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 454 | 5. Who paid each receipt (legal person, entrepreneur or private individual) and what tax was withheld. | Guide: unchanged exact line |

| 455 | 6. Foreign income and foreign tax paid, with proof and the treaty country. | Guide: unchanged exact line |
